### [1] [Memorization In Stable Diffusion Is Unexpectedly Driven by CLIP Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02908)
*Bumjun Kim,Albert No*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现Stable Diffusion中CLIP文本嵌入的<pad> token嵌入因结构复制<eot>嵌入而意外放大记忆效应，并提出两种推理时缓解策略：替换tokenizer默认<pad>并掩码<eot>嵌入，或部分掩码<pad>嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解文本嵌入如何导致文本到图像扩散模型中的记忆化现象，以提升模型的可解释性和安全性。

Method: 将输入token分为<startoftext>, <prompt>, <endoftext>, <pad>四类，分析其CLIP嵌入在生成中的作用；提出两种推理时缓解策略：1) 将tokenizer默认<pad>从<eot>替换为! token并掩码<eot>嵌入；2) 部分掩码<pad>嵌入。

Result: <pad>嵌入因结构复制<eot>嵌入（CLIP训练中唯一显式优化的嵌入）而意外放大其影响，导致模型过度依赖<eot>嵌入，从而驱动记忆化；两种策略均能有效抑制记忆化且不降低生成质量。

Conclusion: 提出两种无需预先检测即可部署的简单有效的记忆化缓解策略，为扩散模型的安全部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Understanding how textual embeddings contribute to memorization in text-to-image diffusion models is crucial for both interpretability and safety. This paper investigates an unexpected behavior of CLIP embeddings in Stable Diffusion, revealing that the model disproportionately relies on specific embeddings. We categorize input tokens as <startoftext>, <prompt>, <endoftext> and <pad> with corresponding embeddings $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{sot}}, \mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{pr}}, \mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{eot}}, \mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{pad}}$. We discover that $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{pr}}$ contribute minimally to generation in memorized cases. In contrast, $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{pad}}$ strongly affect memorization due to their structural duplication of $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{eot}}$, the only embedding explicitly optimized during CLIP training. This duplication unintentionally amplifies the influence of $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{eot}}$, causing the model to over-rely on it, thereby driving memorization. Based on these observations, we propose two simple yet effective inference-time mitigation strategies: (1) Replacing the tokenizer's default <pad> from <eot> to the ! token before embedding, and masking the $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{eot}}$; (2) Partial masking of $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{pad}}$. Both suppress memorization without degrading quality, and are readily deployable without prior detection.

</details>


### [2] [Reasoning-Guided Grounding: Elevating Video Anomaly Detection through Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02912)
*Sakshi Agarwal,Aishik Konwer,Ankit Parag Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VANGUARD是一个将异常分类、空间定位和链式推理统一在单一VLM中的视频异常检测框架，通过三阶段课程学习（分类器预热→LoRA空间定位→链式推理生成）和教师-学生标注流水线，在UCF-Crime上达到94% ROC-AUC和84% F1，同时具备可解释推理和异常对象空间定位能力，零样本迁移至XD-Violence和ShanghaiTech。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VAD方法仅做二分类或离群点检测，缺乏可解释推理和精确空间定位；VLM虽有场景理解能力但空间定位不可靠（容易产生幻觉或几何无效的边界框）。需要一种统一框架同时实现分类、空间定位和可解释推理。

Method: 提出三阶段课程学习策略：(1) 冻结骨干特征上的分类器预热，(2) LoRA适配的空间定位，(3) 链式推理生成。采用教师-学生标注流水线：教师VLM (Qwen3-VL-4B) 基于UCA数据集的标注生成结构化子片段推理轨迹，GroundingDINO提供边界框监督。

Result: 在UCF-Crime上达到94% ROC-AUC和84% F1，同时生成可解释的链式推理和异常对象空间定位。消融实验验证了分阶段训练优于整体优化，结构化推理作为隐式正则化器产生比仅分类微调更均衡的预测。零样本迁移至XD-Violence和ShanghaiTech展现跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: VANGUARD证明了将分类、定位和推理统一在单一VLM框架中的可行性，三阶段课程学习有效缓解了VAD标注稀疏问题，结构化推理不仅提供可解释性还起到了正则化作用，提升了模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) has traditionally been framed as binary classification or outlier detection, providing neither interpretable reasoning nor precise spatial localization of anomalous events. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer rich scene understanding, they struggle with reliable spatial grounding - often producing hallucinated or geometrically invalid bounding boxes when asked to localize objects. We propose VANGUARD (Video Anomaly Understanding through Reasoning and Grounding), a framework that unifies anomaly classification, spatial grounding, and chain-of-thought reasoning within a single VLM. VANGUARD introduces a three-stage curriculum that progressively layers training objectives: (1) classifier warmup on frozen backbone features, (2) LoRA-adapted spatial grounding, and (3) chain-of-thought generation. To overcome the sparse annotation typical of VAD benchmarks, we employ a teacher-student annotation pipeline in which a VLM (Qwen3-VL-4B) generates structured per-subclip reasoning trajectories based on manual annotations available from the UCA Dataset. Further, GroundingDINO provides bounding box supervision. On UCF-Crime, VANGUARD achieves 94% ROC-AUC with 84% F1 while simultaneously producing interpretable chain-of-thought explanations and spatial grounding of anomalous objects - capabilities absent from prior VAD methods. Ablations confirm that staged training outperforms monolithic optimization, and that structured reasoning acts as an implicit regularizer yielding more balanced predictions than classification-only fine-tuning. Zero-shot transfer to XD-Violence and ShanghaiTech demonstrates cross-domain generalization without target-domain adaptation.

</details>


### [3] [Approaching human parity in the quality of automated organoid image segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03053)
*Chase Cartwright,Gongbo Guo,Sai Teja Pusuluri,Christopher N. Mayhew,Mark Hester,Horacio E. Castillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种结合SAM基础模型与领域专用工具的复合方法，用于自动分割iPSC衍生的类器官图像中的球体，在多数条件下达到甚至接近人工标注者间的变异水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类器官发育过程中形态和细胞组织动态变化复杂，需要先进的成像和分析工具来准确监测类器官生长轨迹并研究疾病过程。现有工具在自动测量类器官大小和形状方面精度不足。

Method: 提出一种复合方法，将通用基础模型Segment Anything Model (SAM)与现有的领域专用工具相结合。在类器官图像数据上测试该复合方法与多个现有工具，并与人工分割结果进行比较。

Result: 没有任何单一现有工具能在所有测试条件下实现足够准确的分割；新提出的复合方法在除极少数最具挑战性的图像外，均产生一致且准确的结果。该方法在一种度量指标上达到人工标注者间变异水平，在其他指标上也接近该水平。

Conclusion: 复合方法结合了基础模型的泛化能力和领域工具的专门性，有效提升了类器官图像分割的准确性和一致性，可替代人工标注用于类器官发育监测。

Abstract: Organoids are complex, three dimensional, self-organizing cell cultures which manifest organ-like features and represent a powerful platform for studying human disease and developing treatment options. Organoid development is characterized by dynamic morphological and cellular organization, which mimic some aspects of organ development. To study these rapid changes over the course of organoid development, advanced imaging and analytical tools are critical to accurately monitor the trajectory of organoid growth and investigate disease processes.
  In this work, we focus on computer vision and machine learning techniques to automatically measure the size and shape of developing spheroids derived from pluripotent stem cells (iPSCs), which are typically the starting material for generating organoid cultures. To facilitate this task, we introduce a composite method that combines the Segment Anything Model (SAM), a general-purpose foundation model, with an existing domain-specific tool. This composite method is evaluated together with several existing tools by testing them on organoid image data and comparing with the results of manual image segmentation. We find that no single existing tool is able to segment the test images with sufficient accuracy across all test conditions, but the newly introduced composite method produces consistent and accurate results for all but a very small fraction of the most challenging images. Finally, we compare the accuracy of this method to the variability between manual segmentations by independent annotators (inter-observer variability) and find that by one measure it performs at the level of inter-observer variability and by others it performs very close to it.

</details>


### [4] [Learning to Segment using Summary Statistics and Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03059)
*Omkar Kulkarni,Edward Raff,Tim Oates*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用弱监督信号（少量前景像素）和统计摘要（如面积）训练分割模型的方法，通过新设计的损失函数（结合图像重建、统计匹配和弱监督重叠项），在标准图像、超声和CT数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学专家常手动分割图像用于诊断统计，但随后丢弃标注。为了减轻这一负担，希望利用这些被丢弃的统计信息（如区域面积）训练分割模型，但发现仅靠统计信息不足，需要加入少量弱监督信号（少数前景像素）来提升性能。

Method: 设计了一个新型损失函数，包含三个项：(1) 图像重建质量项，(2) 与统计摘要（如面积）匹配项，(3) 预测前景与弱监督信号（少量标注像素）的重叠项。

Result: 在标准图像、乳腺癌超声图像和肾脏肿瘤CT扫描数据上的实验表明，仅靠统计摘要不足以训练分割模型，加入少量像素的弱监督信号后性能显著提升，验证了方法的有效性和潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法利用临床实践中被丢弃的统计摘要和少量弱标注信息，能够有效训练医学图像分割模型，有望减轻专家手动标注的负担。

Abstract: Medical experts often manually segment images to obtain diagnostic statistics and discard the resulting annotations. We aim to train segmentation models to alleviate this burden, but constrained to the retained summary statistics (e.g., the area of the annotated region). Empirical results suggest that statistics alone are insufficient for this task, but adding weak information in the form of a few pixels within the area of interest significantly improves performance. We use a novel loss function that combines terms for image reconstruction quality, matching to summary statistics, and overlap between the predicted foreground and the weak supervisory signal. Experiments on standard image, ultrasound (breast cancer), and Computed Tomography (CT) scan (kidney tumors) data demonstrate the utility and potential of the approach.

</details>


### [5] [One Sequence to Segment Them All: Efficient Data Augmentation for CT and MRI Cross-Domain 3D Spine Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03098)
*Nathan Molinier,Hendrik Möller,Thomas Dagonneau,Anna Curto-Vilalta,Robert Graf,Matan Atad,Daniel Rueckert,Jan S. Kirschke,Julien Cohen-Adad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对医学图像分割中跨模态泛化问题的数据增强技术，通过在单一模态上训练并在七个分布外数据集上评估，实现了平均Dice提升155%，域内精度仅下降0.008%，并发布了GPU优化的开源工具箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习医学图像分割模型因高质量标注数据稀缺和跨成像协议泛化能力不足而受限，尤其在MRI和CT中，模型通常仅在单一采集序列上训练，对未见序列或对比度的鲁棒性较差。数据增强对跨模态泛化的影响尚未被定量探索。

Method: 设计一组针对性的数据增强技术，在三个单模态/序列数据集上分别训练脊柱分割模型，在七个分布外数据集（CT和MRI）上评估，模拟单序列训练、多序列/对比度/模态部署场景。使用GPU优化增强以缓解计算开销。

Result: 在未见域上平均Dice提升155%，域内精度平均下降仅0.008%，实现CT与MRI间的有效迁移；GPU优化使训练效率提升约10%。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据增强方法能显著提升模型对异质临床成像场景的鲁棒性，且不牺牲训练速度，已作为开源工具箱发布，可无缝集成到nnUNet和MONAI等常用框架中。

Abstract: Deep learning-based medical image segmentation is increasingly used to support clinical diagnosis and develop new treatment strategies. However, model performance remains limited by the scarcity of high-quality annotated data and insufficient generalization across imaging protocols. This limitation is particularly evident in MRI and CT, where models are typically trained on a single acquisition sequence and exhibit reduced robustness when applied to unseen sequences or contrasts. Although data augmentation is widely used to improve general robustness on medical images, its impact on cross-modality generalization has not been quantitatively explored. In this work, we study a targeted set of data augmentation techniques designed to improve cross-modality transfer. We train three spine segmentation models, each on a single-modality/sequence dataset, and evaluate them across seven out-of-distribution datasets (spanning CT and MRI), reflecting a realistic single-sequence training and multi-sequence/contrast/modality deployment scenario. Our results demonstrate substantial performance gains on unseen domains (average Dice gain of 155 %) while preserving in-domain accuracy (average Dice decrease of 0.008 %), including effective transfer between CT and MRI. To mitigate the computational cost typically associated with strong data augmentation, we implement GPU-optimized augmentations that maintain, and even improve, training efficiency by approximately 10 %. We release our approach as an open-source toolbox, enabling seamless integration into commonly used frameworks such as nnUNet and MONAI. These augmentations significantly enhance robustness to heterogeneous clinical imaging scenarios without compromising training speed.

</details>


### [6] [NucEval: A Robust Evaluation Framework for Nuclear Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03144)
*Amirreza Mahbod,Ramona Woitek,Jeanne Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NucEval，一个针对计算病理学中核实例分割的鲁棒评估框架，通过处理模糊区域、分数归一化、重叠实例和边界不确定性四个关键问题，改进了现有评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核实例分割的评估流程长期被忽视，现有方法存在四个关键问题：模糊区域处理不当、分数未归一化、重叠实例未妥善处理、边界不确定性未被考虑，导致评估结果不够鲁棒和准确。

Method: 识别核实例分割评估中的四个关键问题，提出对应的改进方案：模糊区域处理、分数归一化、重叠实例处理、边界不确定性处理，并将这些改进整合为统一框架NucEval。使用NuInsSeg数据集（具有独特特性）及两个外部数据集，结合三个CNN和ViT模型进行验证。

Result: NucEval框架能够对核实例分割进行更鲁棒的评估，通过NuInsSeg和两个外部数据集上的实验，展示了这些修改对实例分割指标的影响。

Conclusion: NucEval为核实例分割提供了更可靠的评估基准，代码和指南已公开，有助于推动计算病理学领域的标准化评估。

Abstract: In computational pathology, nuclear instance segmentation is a fundamental task with many downstream clinical applications. With the advent of deep learning, many approaches, including convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and vision transformers (ViTs), have been proposed for this task, along with both machine learning-based and non-machine learning-based pre- and post-processing techniques to further boost performance. However, one fundamental aspect that has received less attention is the evaluation pipeline. In this study, we identify four key issues associated with nuclear instance segmentation evaluation and propose corresponding solutions. Our proposed modifications, namely handling vague regions, score normalization, overlapping instances, and border uncertainty, are integrated into a unified framework called NucEval, which enables robust evaluation of nuclear instance segmentation. We evaluate this pipeline using the NuInsSeg dataset, which provides unique characteristics that make it particularly suitable for this study, as well as two additional external datasets, with three CNN- and ViT-based nuclear instance segmentation models, to demonstrate the impact of these modifications on instance segmentation metrics. The code, along with complete guidelines and illustrative examples, is publicly available at: https://github.com/masih4/nuc_eval.

</details>


### [7] [Boundary-Aware Uncertainty Quantification for Wildfire Spread Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03148)
*Jonas V. Funk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出火中心评估区域（FCER）框架，用于在野火蔓延预测中实现空间条件化的不确定性量化评估，并对比了集成模型与蒸馏单通道学生模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在野火蔓延预测中缺乏原则性的不确定性量化，且全局指标无法充分评估边界敏感场景（如野火蔓延）的预测质量，亟需面向操作需求的评估方法。

Method: 提出FCER框架作为一种空间条件化协议，用于在关键火区内表征不确定性量化；基于WildfireSpreadTS数据集，对比集成模型与蒸馏单通道学生模型在FCER框架下的表现。

Result: 学生模型在边界相关区域具有与集成模型相当的校准性能，并在不确定性排序上表现出互补性。

Conclusion: FCER框架能够将UQ评估转向更具操作相关性的方向，蒸馏模型在计算效率与不确定性质量之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: Reliable wildfire spread prediction is vital for risk-aware emergency planning, yet most deep learning models lack principled uncertainty quantification (UQ). Further, for boundary-sensitive cases like wildfire spread, evaluating models with global metrics alone is often insufficient. To shift the focus of UQ evaluation toward a more operationally relevant approach, the Fire-Centered Evaluation Region (FCER) framework is introduced as a spatially conditioned protocol to characterize UQ within critical fire zones. Using FCER, an Ensemble is compared against an distilled single-pass student model on the WildfireSpreadTS dataset. The student model demonstrates comparable calibration and complementary uncertainty ranking in boundary-relevant regimes. Code is available at https://github. com/jonasvilhofunk/WildfireUQ-FCER

</details>


### [8] [Sentinel2Cap: A Human-Annotated Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Remote Sensing Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03189)
*Lucrezia Tosato,Gianluca Lombardi,Ronny Hansch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sentinel2Cap，一个包含Sentinel-1 SAR和Sentinel-2多光谱图像的人工标注多模态遥感描述数据集，并使用Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型在零样本设置下评估了不同模态的图像描述性能，发现RGB图像效果最好，SAR图像最具挑战性，而模态感知提示能提升所有指标的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感图像描述数据集多集中于自然图像或高分辨率光学遥感图像，缺乏面向中等分辨率多模态卫星数据（尤其是SAR和光学数据）的人工标注描述数据集，限制了跨模态场景理解的研究。

Method: 构建了Sentinel2Cap数据集，包含10米和20米空间分辨率的Sentinel-1 SAR和Sentinel-2多光谱图像块，覆盖多种土地覆盖类型，并经过人工标注与严格校验。采用Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型在零样本设置下对RGB、多光谱、SAR伪RGB三种模态进行图像描述评估。

Result: RGB图像在零样本描述任务中表现最佳，SAR图像对视觉-语言模型更具挑战性；提供模态感知的上下文提示能一致性地提升所有评估指标的性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明多模态遥感图像描述仍面临挑战，尤其是SAR模态；人工标注数据集对推动跨模态场景理解研究具有重要价值；所有数据已公开。

Abstract: Image captioning has become an important task in computer vision, enabling models to generate natural language descriptions of visual content. While several datasets exist for natural images and high-resolution optical remote sensing imagery, the availability of captioning datasets for multimodal satellite data remains limited, particularly for SAR imagery and medium-resolution sensors. We introduce Sentinel2Cap, a human-annotated multimodal captioning dataset containing Sentinel-1 SAR and Sentinel-2 multi-spectral image patches at 10 m and 20 m spatial resolution with diverse land cover compositions. Captions are created manually and carefully validated to ensure both semantic accuracy and linguistic quality. To evaluate Sentinel2Cap, we perform a zero-shot captioning using the Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct model across three image modalities: RGB, multi-spectral, and SAR pseudo-RGB representations. Results show that RGB images achieve the highest captioning performance, while SAR images remain more challenging for vision-language models. Providing modality-specific contextual prompts consistently improves performance across all metrics. These findings highlight both the challenges of multimodal remote sensing image captioning and the potential value of human-annotated datasets for advancing research in cross-modal scene understanding. All the material is publicly avaiable.

</details>


### [9] [Synthetic Data Generation for Long-Tail Medical Image Classification: A Case Study in Skin Lesions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03221)
*Jiaxiang Jiang,Mahesh Subedar,Omesh Tickoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的合成数据增强管道，通过新颖的修复扩散模型和OOD后选择机制，为医学长尾分类生成多样化、真实且临床有意义的合成样本。在ISIC2019数据集上，该方法在尾部类别上取得了超过28%的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学多类数据集普遍存在长尾分布问题，罕见类别往往对应高风险疾病，但现有方法（如专用架构、重平衡损失函数、手工数据增强）改进有限且难以扩展。因此需要一种能生成多样化、真实合成数据的方法来缓解长尾不平衡问题。

Method: 提出基于扩散模型的合成数据增强管道，核心包括：(1) 新颖的修复扩散模型用于生成合成样本；(2) 结合Out-of-Distribution (OOD)后选择机制，确保生成样本的多样性、真实性和临床意义。

Result: 在ISIC2019皮肤病变分类数据集上评估，该方法在整体性能上取得显著提升，特别是在尾部类别上，样本最少的类别提升了超过28%。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的增强方法在缓解长尾不平衡问题、提升医学分类鲁棒性方面是有效的，为长尾医学分类提供了一种可行的合成数据解决方案。

Abstract: Long-tailed class distributions are pervasive in multi-class medical datasets and pose significant challenges for deep learning models which typically underperform on tail classes with limited samples. This limitation is particularly problematic in medical applications, where rare classes often correspond to severe or high-risk diseases and therefore require high diagnostic accuracy. Existing solutions-including specialized architectures, rebalanced loss functions, and handcrafted data augmentation-offer only marginal improvements and struggle to scale due to their limited and largely deterministic variability. To address these challenges, we introduce a diffusion-model-driven synthetic data augmentation pipeline tailored for medical long-tailed classification. Our approach features a novel inpainting diffusion model combined with an Out-of-Distribution (OOD) post-selection mechanism to ensure diverse, realistic, and clinically meaningful synthetic samples. Evaluated on the ISIC2019 skin lesion classification dataset, one of the largest and most imbalanced medical imaging benchmarks, our method yields substantial improvements in overall performance, with particularly pronounced gains on tail classes with more than $28\%$ improvement on the class with the fewest samples. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of diffusion-based augmentation in mitigating long-tail imbalance and enhancing medical classification robustness.

</details>


### [10] [VEBench:Benchmarking Large Multimodal Models for Real-World Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03276)
*Andong Deng,Dawei Du,Zhenfang Chen,Wen Zhong,Fan Chen,Guang Chen,Chia-Wen Kuo,Longyin Wen,Chen Chen,Sijie Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEBENCH是首个评估多视频编辑理解与操作推理的综合基准，包含3.9K高质量编辑视频和3,080个QA对，通过技术识别和操作模拟两项任务揭示当前大模型与人类编辑认知之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型多模态模型在通用视频理解方面取得了进展，但其在多视频推理和编辑操作工作流中的能力尚未被探索，缺乏系统性的评估基准来衡量模型的编辑知识理解和操作推理能力。

Method: 通过三轮人机协作标注流程构建包含3.9K高质量编辑视频（超过257小时）和3,080个人工验证QA对的基准；设计两项互补任务：1) 视频编辑技术识别（7种编辑技术）；2) 视频编辑操作模拟（从多个候选片段中选择并定位相关片段）。

Result: 在专有模型（如Gemini-2.5-Pro）和开源LMM上的大量实验表明，当前模型性能与人类编辑认知之间存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: 亟需弥合视频理解与创造性操作推理之间的鸿沟，VEBENCH可作为推进智能视频编辑系统和复杂推理研究的基础。

Abstract: Real-world video editing demands not only expert knowledge of cinematic techniques but also multimodal reasoning to select, align, and combine footage into coherent narratives. While recent Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have shown remarkable progress in general video understanding, their abilities in multi-video reasoning and operational editing workflows remain largely unexplored. We introduce VEBENCH, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate both editing knowledge understanding and operational reasoning in realistic video editing scenarios. VEBENCH contains 3.9K high-quality edited videos (over 257 hours) and 3,080 human-verified QA pairs, built through a three-round human-AI collaborative annotation pipeline that ensures precise temporal labeling and semantic consistency. It features two complementary QA tasks: 1) Video Editing Technique Recognition, assessing models' ability to identify 7 editing techniques using multimodal cues; and 2) Video Editing Operation Simulation, modeling real-world editing workflows by requiring the selection and temporal localization of relevant clips from multiple candidates. Extensive experiments across proprietary (e.g., Gemini-2.5-Pro) and open-source LMMs reveal a large gap between current model performance and human-level editing cognition. These results highlight the urgent need for bridging video understanding with creative operational reasoning. We envision VEBENCH as a foundation for advancing intelligent video editing systems and driving future research on complex reasoning.

</details>


### [11] [FACTOR: Counterfactual Training-Free Test-Time Adaptation for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03294)
*Kaixiang Zhao,Mao Ye,Lihua Zhou,Hu Wang,Luping Ji,Song Tang,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FACTOR提出了一种基于反事实推理的无训练测试时自适应框架，通过扰动测试图像的非因果属性并比较区域级预测，在不更新参数的情况下抑制属性依赖的预测，有效提升了开放词汇目标检测在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇目标检测在分布偏移下容易因非因果视觉属性（如亮度、纹理）与目标类别之间的虚假相关性而失败，现有测试时自适应方法要么依赖昂贵的在线优化，要么进行全局校准，忽略了这些失败的属性特定性质。

Method: 提出FACTOR框架，通过扰动测试图像的非因果属性，生成反事实视图，然后比较原始视图与反事实视图之间的区域级预测，量化属性敏感性、语义相关性和预测变异性，从而选择性地抑制属性依赖的预测，整个过程无需参数更新。

Result: 在PASCAL-C、COCO-C和FoggyCityscapes数据集上，FACTOR一致优于先前的测试时自适应方法。

Conclusion: 显式的反事实推理能有效提升开放词汇目标检测在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，并且该方法轻量、无需训练，具有实用性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection often fails under distribution shifts, as it can be misled by spurious correlations between non-causal visual attributes (e.g., brightness, texture) and object categories. Existing test-time adaptation (TTA) methods either depend on costly online optimization or perform global calibration, overlooking the attribute-specific nature of these failures. To address this, we propose FACTOR (counterFACtual training-free Test-time adaptation for Open-vocabulaRy object detection), a lightweight framework grounded in counterfactual reasoning. By perturbing test images along non-causal attributes and comparing region-level predictions between original and counterfactual views, FACTOR quantifies attribute sensitivity, semantic relevance, and prediction variation to selectively suppress attribute-dependent predictions-without parameter updates. Experiments on PASCAL-C, COCO-C, and FoggyCityscapes show that FACTOR consistently outperforms prior TTA methods, demonstrating that explicit counterfactual reasoning effectively improves robustness under distribution shifts.

</details>


### [12] [TACO: Trajectory Aligning Cross-view Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03315)
*Tavis Shore,Oscar Mendez,Simon Hadfield*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TACO提出了一个紧耦合IMU与细粒度跨视角地理定位的管线，仅需启动时一次GNSS读数，之后完全依赖机载传感器。通过闭环形式的跨轨误差模型触发CVGL定位，前向偏置的五点多裁剪搜索保持推理成本固定，偏航残差门控拒绝与罗盘不一致的定位结果，各向异性体坐标系噪声模型根据每次定位置信度缩放UKF更新，因子图提供离线平滑轨迹。在KITTI数据集上，中位ATE从97.0米（纯IMU）降至16.3米，降低5.9倍，每帧融合成本<0.1ms，相机占空比5-10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有细粒度CVGL方法仅作为一次性定位器评估，从未作为实时管线中的主定位源；IMU提供高帧率相对运动但累积无界漂移，缺乏绝对锚点。将两者紧耦合以在GNSS退化场景下提供持续稳定的绝对定位。

Method: 1) 闭环跨轨误差模型：在IMU漂移超过匹配捕获半径前触发CVGL；2) 前向偏置五点多裁剪搜索：固定五次前向推理的搜索策略；3) 偏航残差门控：拒绝与机载罗盘不一致的定位结果；4) 各向异性体坐标系噪声模型：根据每次定位置信度缩放无迹卡尔曼滤波（UKF）更新；5) 因子图：结合经过验证的闭环检测提供离线平滑轨迹。

Result: 在KITTI原始数据集上，中位ATE从纯IMU的97.0米降至16.3米（5.9倍提升），每帧融合成本<0.1ms，相机占空比仅5-10%。

Conclusion: TACO成功实现了IMU与细粒度CVGL的紧耦合，在极低的计算成本和相机使用率下大幅降低了定位误差，为GNSS退化环境提供了实用的替代定位方案。

Abstract: Cross-View Geo-localisation (CVGL) matches ground imagery against satellite tiles to give absolute position fixes, an alternative to GNSS where signals are occluded, jammed, or spoofed. Recent fine-grained CVGL methods regress sub-tile metric pose, but have only been evaluated as one-shot localisers, never as the primary fix in a live pipeline. Inertial sensing provides high-rate relative motion, but accumulates unbounded drift without an absolute anchor. We propose TACO, a tightly-coupled IMU + fine-grained CVGL pipeline that consumes a single GNSS reading at start-up and thereafter operates on onboard sensing alone. A closed-form cross-track error model triggers CVGL before IMU drift exceeds the matcher's capture radius, and a forward-biased five-point multi-crop search keeps inference cost fixed at five forward passes per fix. A yaw-residual gate rejects fixes that disagree with the onboard compass, and an anisotropic body-frame noise model scales each Unscented Kalman Filter update by per-fix confidence. A factor graph with vetted loop closures provides an offline smoothed trajectory. On the KITTI raw dataset, TACO reduces median Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) from 97.0m (IMU-only) to 16.3m, a 5.9 times reduction, at <0.1 ms per-frame fusion cost and a 5-10% camera duty cycle. Code is available: github.com/tavisshore/TACO.

</details>


### [13] [AHPA: Adaptive Hierarchical Prior Alignment for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03317)
*Ruibin Min,Yexin Liu,Aimin Pan,Changsheng Lu,Jiafei Wu,Kelu Yao,Xiaogang Xu,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AHPA是一种自适应分层先验对齐框架，通过利用冻结VAE编码器中天然存在的多层次特征，并结合时间步条件动态路由器，在扩散Transformer训练中根据信噪比动态调整对齐粒度，从而解决传统静态对齐方法在高低噪声阶段表示不匹配的问题，提升收敛速度与生成质量且无额外推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示对齐方法在整个去噪轨迹中采用固定的监督目标或固定的对齐粒度（无论是外部视觉编码器、内部自表示还是VAE特征），无法适应不同信噪比阶段模型对表示粒度的不同需求——高噪声阶段需要粗粒度语义和布局锚定，低噪声阶段需要细粒度空间细节和结构保真度，导致表示不匹配。

Method: 提出AHPA框架：1) 利用冻结VAE编码器中天然存在的多层特征（从局部几何、空间拓扑到粗粒度语义布局）作为互补先验；2) 设计时间步条件动态路由器（Dynamic Router），沿去噪轨迹自适应选择和加权这些分层先验，使对齐粒度与模型动态训练需求同步。

Result: 大量实验表明AHPA相比基线方法在收敛速度和生成质量上均有提升，训练过程中无需外部编码器监督，且推理阶段不引入额外计算成本。

Conclusion: 时间步感知的动态分层对齐策略比静态单级别监督更适用于扩散Transformer训练，利用VAE内部多层次特征即可实现高效对齐，无需依赖外部视觉编码器。

Abstract: Representation alignment has recently emerged as an effective paradigm for accelerating Diffusion Transformer training. Despite their success, existing alignment methods typically impose a fixed supervision target or a fixed alignment granularity throughout the entire denoising trajectory, whether the guidance is provided by external vision encoders, internal self-representations, or VAE-derived features. We argue that such timestep-agnostic alignment is suboptimal because the useful granularity of representation supervision changes systematically with the signal-to-noise ratio. In high-noise regimes, diffusion models benefit more from coarse semantic and layout-level anchoring, whereas in low-noise regimes, the training signal should emphasize spatially detailed and structurally faithful refinement. This non-stationary alignment behavior creates a representational mismatch for static single-level supervisors. To address this issue, we propose Adaptive Hierarchical Prior Alignment (AHPA), a lightweight alignment framework that exploits the hierarchical representations naturally embedded in the frozen VAE encoder. Instead of using only a single compressed latent as the alignment target, AHPA extracts multi-level VAE features that provide complementary priors ranging from local geometry and spatial topology to coarse semantic layout. A timestep-conditioned Dynamic Router adaptively selects and weights these hierarchical priors along the denoising trajectory, thereby synchronizing the alignment granularity with the model's evolving training needs. Extensive experiments show that AHPA improves convergence and generation quality over baselines and incurs no additional inference cost while avoiding external encoder supervision during training.

</details>


### [14] [FreeTimeGS++: Secrets of Dynamic Gaussian Splatting and Their Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03337)
*Lucas Yunkyu Lee,Soonho Kim,Youngwook Kim,Sangmin Kim,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）框架进行了系统的隐藏因素分析，建立了FreeTimeGS_ours控制基线，揭示了高斯持续时间导致的时态分区现象以及光度保真度与时空一致性之间的差异，并在此基础上提出FreeTimeGS++方法，通过门控边缘化和神经速度场实现了更优的稳定性和鲁棒动态表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4DGS虽在动态场景重建中表现优异，但驱动这些性能提升的具体因素尚不明确，缺乏对底层原理的系统性理解，亟需对隐藏因素进行深入分析。

Method: 首先通过形式化并复现FreeTimeGS启发式方法建立控制基线FreeTimeGS_ours；然后沿着4DGS的基本轴对其进行解构分析，揭示关键隐藏因素；最后提出FreeTimeGS++方法，采用门控边缘化和神经速度场提升稳定性与动态表示鲁棒性。

Result: 发现了高斯持续时间导致的时态分区涌现现象，以及光度保真度与时空一致性之间的差异。FreeTimeGS++实现了可重复的结果，降低了运行间方差。

Conclusion: 通过系统性分析揭示了4DGS框架中隐藏的关键因素，提出的FreeTimeGS++在稳定性和动态表示方面表现更优，并将开源实现以推动未来4DGS研究。

Abstract: The recent surge in 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) has achieved impressive dynamic scene reconstruction. While these methods demonstrate remarkable performance, the specific drivers behind such gains remain less explored, making a systematic understanding of the underlying principles challenging. In this paper, we perform a comprehensive analysis of these hidden factors to provide a clearer perspective on the 4DGS framework. We first establish a controlled baseline, FreeTimeGS_ours, by formalizing and reproducing the heuristics of the state-of-the-art FreeTimeGS. Using this framework, we dissect 4DGS along its fundamental axes and uncover key secrets, including the emergent temporal partitioning driven by Gaussian durations and the discrepancy between photometric fidelity and spatiotemporal consistency. Based on these insights, we propose FreeTimeGS++, a principled method that employs gated marginalization and neural velocity fields to achieve superior stability and robust dynamic representations. Our approach yields reproducible results with reduced run-to-run variance. We will release our implementation to provide a reliable foundation for future 4DGS research.

</details>


### [15] [MedSR-Vision: Deep Learning Framework for Multi-Domain Medical Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03343)
*Subhash Gurappa,Trivikram Satharasi,Yashas Hariprasad,Sundararaj Sitharama Iyengar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MedSR-Vision，一个统一的深度学习框架，用于在五种医学影像模态（脑部MRI、胸部X光、肾脏超声、肾结石CT、脊柱MRI）上评估和比较超分辨率模型（SRCNN、SwinIR、Real-ESRGAN），揭示了不同模型在不同放大倍数下的性能优劣，为临床影像工作流中的模型选择提供了指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像超分辨率在提升诊断精度方面至关重要，但现有研究面临解剖结构保真度、感知质量和跨模态泛化能力的挑战，亟需一个统一的评估框架来系统比较不同模型在多模态医学影像上的表现。

Method: 提出了MedSR-Vision统一框架，在五种医学影像模态（MRI、CT、X光、超声、眼底）上，以×2、×3、×4三种放大倍数，对SRCNN、SwinIR和Real-ESRGAN三种代表性模型进行基准测试，使用多种定量指标（涵盖保真度、感知真实性和锐度）进行评估。

Result: Real-ESRGAN在高倍数下感知质量和边缘恢复最优；SwinIR在保持结构和诊断特征方面表现最佳；SRCNN在低倍数下提供高效稳定的性能。

Conclusion: 建立了领域特定的模型选择见解和实用指南，为未来医学图像超分辨率研究和部署提供了标准化的评估框架。

Abstract: Medical image super-resolution (MedSR) is essential for improving diagnostic precision across diverse imaging modalities such as MRI, CT, X-ray, Ultrasound, and Fundus imaging. Despite rapid advances in deep learning, challenges remain in preserving anatomical accuracy, maintaining perceptual quality, and generalizing across medical domains. This paper presents MedSR-Vision, a novel unified deep learning framework for evaluating and comparing super-resolution models across five modalities: Brain MRI, Chest X-ray, Renal Ultrasound, Nephrolithiasis CT, and Spine MRI, at magnification scales of $\times2$, $\times3$, and $\times4$. Three representative models namely SRCNN, SwinIR, and Real-ESRGAN are benchmarked using multiple quantitative metrics encompassing fidelity, perceptual realism, and sharpness.
  Experimental analysis demonstrates that Real-ESRGAN achieves superior perceptual quality and edge recovery at higher scales, SwinIR excels in preserving structural and diagnostic features, and SRCNN provides efficient and stable performance at lower magnifications. The results establish domain-specific insights and practical guidelines for model selection in clinical imaging workflows, offering a standardized evaluation framework for future medical image super-resolution research and deployment.

</details>


### [16] [VLMaxxing through FrameMogging Training-Free Anti-Recomputation for Video Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03351)
*JF Bastien,Sam D'Amico*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出训练无关的抗重计算策略：在视觉-语言模型（VLM）中，通过状态复用避免对视频中未变化场景的冗余计算。在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct-4bit上，同视频连续问答的延迟降低14.90-35.92倍；C-VISION剪枝首次查询前视觉编码任务，实现1.316x加速。同时提出C-CEILING作为端到端加速核算的监管指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频VLM在每一帧或每一次查询时都重复计算视觉状态，即使场景在视频流中已明确保持稳定（如静止的墙面），导致大量冗余计算浪费。作者希望消除这种不必要的"重计算"，让模型只在需要时重新获取视觉证据。

Method: 提出训练无关的抗重计算框架：1) 自适应状态复用：验证前一帧状态是否仍然有效（场景未变、查询相关、缓存拓扑未变），有效则复用，无效则重新提取；2) C-VISION：跳过首次回答前的视觉编码工作；3) C-CEILING：核算单个组件加速在多组件流水线中的实际端到端收益。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct-4bit上同视频多轮问答：延迟降低14.90-35.92倍，93项VideoMME广度测试中保持配对准确率。C-VISION在Gemma 4-E4B-4bit上实现1.316x首次查询加速，无配对漂移或解析失败。50轮重复问答无性能衰减。

Conclusion: 视频VLM中存在大量可通过状态复用消除的冗余计算，训练无关的抗重计算策略能显著降低延迟而不牺牲准确性。更长远的方向是设计原生支持视频媒体（变化、运动、不确定性、对象状态、传感器时间、活动区域）的VLM架构，避免模型从密集RGB帧中重新"发现"世界。

Abstract: Video vision-language models (VLMs) keep paying for visual state the stream already told us was stable. The factory wall did not move, but most VLM pipelines still hand the model dense RGB frames or a fresh prefix again. We study that waste as training-free anti-recomputation: reuse state when validation says it survives, and buy fresh evidence when the scene, query, or cache topology requires it.
  The largest measured win is after ingest. On frozen Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct-4bit, adaptive same-video follow-up reuse preserves paired choices and correctness on a 93-query VideoMME breadth setting while reducing follow-up latency by 14.90-35.92x. The first query is still cold; the win starts when later questions reuse the same video state. Stress tests bound the result: repeated-question schedules hold through 50 turns, while dense-answer-anchored prompt variation separates conservative fixed K=1 repair from faster aggressive policies that drift.
  Fresh-video pruning is smaller but real. C-VISION skips timed vision-tower work before the first answer is generated. On Gemma 4-E4B-4bit, the clean 32f short cell reaches 1.316x first-query speedup with no paired drift or parse failures on 20 items; Qwen shows the fidelity/speed boundary.
  Stage-share ceiling (C-CEILING) is the accounting guardrail: a component speedup becomes an end-to-end speedup only in proportion to the wall-clock share it accelerates, so C-VISION and after-ingest follow-up reuse do not multiply. Candidate C-STREAM remains a native-rate target, not a headline result here. The broader direction is VLM-native media that expose change, motion, uncertainty, object state, sensor time, and active tiles directly, so models do not have to rediscover the world from dense RGB every frame.

</details>


### [17] [Can Multimodal Large Language Models Understand Pathologic Movements? A Pilot Study on Seizure Semiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03352)
*Lina Zhang,Tonmoy Monsoor,Mehmet Efe Lorasdagi,Prateik Sinha,Chong Han,Peizheng Li,Yuan Wang,Jessica Pasqua,Colin McCrimmon,Rajarshi Mazumder,Vwani Roychowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了多模态大语言模型在癫痫发作视频中自动识别病理运动的能力。MLLMs在零样本条件下 outperforms 传统CNN和ViT基线模型（13/18特征），并通过定向信号增强（面部裁剪、姿态估计、音频去噪）进一步提升了10/20特征的性能。94.3%的正确预测解释获得了至少60%的忠实度分数，与癫痫专科医师的推理一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs在识别日常人类活动方面表现强大，但其在神经系统疾病临床相关不自主运动的分析潜力尚未被充分探索。本研究旨在填补这一空白，评估MLLMs在癫痫发作视频中自动识别病理运动的可行性。

Method: 在90个临床癫痫发作记录上，评估了SOTA MLLMs对20个ILAE定义的半学特征的零样本性能，并与微调后的CNN和ViT基线模型进行对比。同时采用特征定向信号增强（面部裁剪、姿态估计、音频去噪）进行性能优化，并通过专家评估MLLM生成解释的忠实度。

Result: MLLMs在无任务特定训练条件下，在18个特征中的13个上优于传统基线模型，特别擅长识别显著的姿势和上下文特征，但在细微、高频运动方面表现不足。信号增强方法改善了20个特征中的10个。94.3%的正确预测解释获得了至少60%的忠实度分数。

Conclusion: 通用MLLMs通过定向预处理策略可适配于专业临床视频分析，为可解释、高效的诊断辅助提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated robust capabilities in recognizing everyday human activities, yet their potential for analyzing clinically significant involuntary movements in neurological disorders remains largely unexplored. This pilot study evaluates the capability of MLLMs for automated recognition of pathological movements in seizure videos. We assessed the zero-shot performance of state-of-the-art MLLMs on 20 ILAE-defined semiological features across 90 clinical seizure recordings. MLLMs outperformed fine-tuned Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) and Vision Transformer (ViT) baseline models on 13 of 18 features without task-specific training, demonstrating particular strength in recognizing salient postural and contextual features while struggling with subtle, high-frequency movements. Feature-targeted signal enhancement (facial cropping, pose estimation, audio denoising) improved performance on 10 of 20 features. Expert evaluation showed that 94.3 percent of MLLM-generated explanations for correctly predicted cases achieved at least 60 percent faithfulness scores, aligning with epileptologist reasoning. These findings demonstrate the potential of adapting general-purpose MLLMs for specialized clinical video analysis through targeted preprocessing strategies, offering a path toward interpretable, efficient diagnostic assistance. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LinaZhangUCLA/PathMotionMLLM.

</details>


### [18] [Tracing Like a Clinician: Anatomy-Guided Spatial Priors for Cephalometric Landmark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03358)
*Sidhartha Mohapatra,Pallavi Mohanty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出五阶段解剖指导初始化管线，将正畸医生的头影测量工作流转化为计算操作，生成置信度加权空间注意力先验用于HRNet-W32检测器。在1502张X光片上对25个标志点达到1.04mm平均径向误差，超越先前SOTA（1.23mm/19标志点）15.4%。消融实验表明：去除解剖先验不仅减慢收敛，更破坏泛化能力（验证集收敛相近但测试集1.94 vs 1.04mm）；替换为随机高斯先验泛化更差（2.24mm），证实改进源于解剖正确位置而非额外输入通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正畸医生追踪头影测量片时遵循结构化工作流（识别软组织轮廓、分区、描边、基于几何定义定位标志点），但现有自动化系统无法复现这一推理过程。

Method: 提出五阶段解剖指导初始化管线，将临床工作流转化为计算操作，生成置信度加权空间注意力先验，输入至HRNet-W32检测器进行下游检测。

Result: 在来自3个来源、7+设备的1502张X光片上，对25个标志点达到1.04mm平均径向误差，超越先前SOTA（1.23mm/19标志点）15.4%，其中12个标志点低于1mm。三路对照消融实验揭示解剖先验的关键作用。

Conclusion: 临床领域知识编码为空间先验提供了架构和数据增强本身无法提供的归纳偏置，对泛化能力至关重要。

Abstract: When orthodontists trace cephalometric radiographs, they follow a structured workflow: identify the soft tissue profile, partition the skull into anatomical regions, trace contours, and locate landmarks using geometric definitions -- yet no automated system replicates this reasoning. We present a five-phase anatomy-guided initialization pipeline that translates this clinical workflow into computational operations, producing confidence-weighted spatial attention priors for a downstream HRNet-W32 detector. On 1,502 radiographs from three sources spanning 7+ imaging devices, the system achieves 1.04 mm mean radial error on 25 landmarks -- surpassing prior state-of-the-art (1.23 mm on 19 landmarks) by 15.4%, with twelve landmarks below 1 mm. A three-way controlled ablation reveals two striking findings. First, removing anatomical priors does not merely slow convergence -- it destroys generalization: both models converge to ~1.03 mm on validation, but diverge to 1.94 vs. 1.04 mm on the test set. Second, replacing anatomical priors with random-position Gaussians produces even worse generalization (2.24 mm), confirming that the improvement derives from anatomically correct positioning, not additional input channels. Clinical domain knowledge encoded as spatial priors provides an inductive bias that architecture and data augmentation alone do not provide.

</details>


### [19] [Mix3R: Mixing Feed-forward Reconstruction and Generative 3D Priors for Joint Multi-view Aligned 3D Reconstruction and Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03359)
*Siyou Lin,Zhou Xue,Hongwen Zhang,Liang An,Dongping Li,Shaohui Jiao,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mix3R提出了一种混合前馈重建与3D生成的统一框架，通过两阶段生成（稀疏体素生成+纹理几何生成）和混合Transformer架构，在保留预训练先验的同时实现更好的2D-3D对齐，相比纯生成方法具有更优的输入对齐，相比前馈重建方法具有更精确的相机位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前稀疏视角3D重建存在两条路径：前馈重建能预测像素对齐的点图但缺乏完整几何，生成式3D重建能生成完整几何但输入对齐性差。作者旨在将两者优势融合，在单一框架中实现既有完整几何又有良好输入对齐的3D重建。

Method: Mix3R采用两阶段生成架构：(1) 稀疏体素生成阶段，通过混合Transformer架构（MoT）联合生成粗粒度3D结构（稀疏体素）、每视角点图和相机参数；(2) 纹理几何生成阶段，基于第一阶段生成的稀疏体素和点图计算基于重叠的注意力偏置，以无训练方式注入预训练的纹理几何生成模型，实现输入纹理的正确放置。MoT架构在前馈重建模型和3D生成模型中插入全局自注意力，保留预训练先验的同时增强2D-3D对齐。

Result: Mix3R生成的3D形状相比纯3D生成方法具有更好的输入对齐性，同时相机位姿估计比前馈重建方法更精确。前馈分支学会将预测锚定到生成式3D先验上，3D生成分支则从前馈分支获得几何信息丰富的特征条件。

Conclusion: Mix3R成功融合了前馈重建和3D生成的优势，通过混合Transformer架构和两阶段生成策略，实现了完整几何与精确输入对齐的统一，为稀疏视角3D重建提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent trends in sparse-view 3D reconstruction have taken two different paths: feed-forward reconstruction that predicts pixel-aligned point maps without a complete geometry, and generative 3D reconstruction that generates complete geometry but often with poor input-alignment. We present Mix3R, a novel generative 3D reconstruction method which mixes feed-forward reconstruction and 3D generation into a single framework in an aligned manner. Mix3R generates a 3D shape in two stages: a sparse voxel generation stage and a textured geometry generation stage. Unlike pure generative methods, our first-stage generation jointly produces a coarse 3D structure (sparse voxels), per-view point maps and camera parameters aligned to that 3D structure. This is made possible by introducing a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that inserts global self-attentions to a feed-forward reconstruction model and a 3D generative model, both pretrained on large-scale data. This design effectively retains the pretrained priors but enables better 2D-3D alignment. Based on the initial aligned generations of sparse 3D voxels and point maps, we compute an overlap-based attention bias that is directly added to another pretrained textured geometry generation model, enabling it to correctly place input textures onto generated shapes in a training-free manner. Our design brings mutual benefits to both feed-forward reconstruction and 3D generation: The feed-forward branch learns to ground its predictions to a generative 3D prior, and conversely, the 3D generation branch is conditioned on geometrically informative features from the feed-forward branch. As a result, our method produces 3D shapes with better input alignment compared with pure 3D generative methods, together with camera pose estimations more accurate than previous feed-forward reconstruction methods. Our project page is at https://jsnln.github.io/mix3r/

</details>


### [20] [Dynamic Distillation and Gradient Consistency for Robust Long-Tailed Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03364)
*Taigo Sakai,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对长尾类别增量学习（LT-CIL）提出梯度一致性正则化和基于归一化熵的自适应蒸馏损失权重调整，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘和类别不平衡问题，在多个基准上实现了最高5.0%的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LT-CIL同时面临连续学习中固有的灾难性遗忘以及长尾分布带来的少数类欠学习与多数类过拟合双重挑战，现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 梯度一致性正则化：利用梯度移动平均抑制训练过程中的剧烈波动；2) 动态调整蒸馏损失权重：通过归一化熵衡量类别不平衡程度，自适应平衡旧知识保留与新知识获取。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-LT、ImageNetSubset-LT和Food101-LT基准上，一致实现最高5.0%的准确率提升；在任务从多数类向少数类推进的"In-ordered"困难设定下表现尤为突出；计算开销无明显增加。

Conclusion: 所提框架在不显著增加计算开销的前提下有效缓解了LT-CIL中的灾难性遗忘与类别不平衡问题，具有良好的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The task of Long-tailed Class Incremental Learning (LT-CIL) addresses the sequential learning of new classes from datasets with imbalanced class distributions. This scenario intensifies the fundamental problem of catastrophic forgetting, inherent to continual learning, with the dual challenges of under-learning minority classes and overfitting majority classes. To tackle these combined issues, this paper proposes two main techniques. First, we introduce gradient consistency regularization, which leverages the moving average of gradients to suppress abrupt fluctuations and stabilize the training process. Second, we dynamically adjust the weight of the distillation loss by measuring the degree of class imbalance with normalized entropy. This adaptive weighting establishes an optimal balance between retaining old knowledge and acquiring new information. Experiments on the CIFAR-100-LT, ImageNetSubset-LT, and Food101-LT benchmarks show that our method achieves consistent accuracy improvements of up to 5.0\%. Furthermore, we demonstrate dramatic gains in the challenging 'In-ordered' setting, where tasks progress from majority to minority classes, highlighting our method's robustness in mitigating forgetting under unfavorable learning dynamics. This enhanced performance is achieved without a significant increase in computational overhead, demonstrating the practicality of our framework.

</details>


### [21] [Dual-Foundation Models for Unsupervised Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03365)
*Yerin Cheon,Aruna Balasubramanian,Francois Rameau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种双基础模型UDA框架，利用SAM（超像素引导提示）和DINOv3（稳定类原型）来解决语义分割无监督域适应中伪标签高置信度偏差和原型初始化偏差问题，在GTA→Cityscapes和SYNTHIA→Cityscapes上分别提升+1.3%和+1.4% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UDA方法受限于：(1) 依赖高置信度伪标签，仅学习目标域的子集；(2) 基于原型的对比方法从源域训练模型初始化类原型，导致有偏且不稳定的锚点。因此需要更全面的目标域像素学习和更稳定的类原型表示。

Method: 提出双基础模型UDA框架：(1) 使用SAM结合超像素引导提示，使模型能从目标域更广泛的像素（不限于高置信度预测）中学习；(2) 引入DINOv3，利用其鲁棒的表示学习能力构建稳定、域不变的类原型。

Result: 在GTA→Cityscapes上提升+1.3% mIoU，在SYNTHIA→Cityscapes上提升+1.4% mIoU，超越强UDA基线。

Conclusion: 双基础模型策略有效解决了UDA中伪标签偏差和原型不稳定问题，为域适应语义分割提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation provides pixel-level scene understanding essential for autonomous driving and fine-grained perception tasks. However, training segmentation models requires costly, labor-intensive annotations on real-world datasets. Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA) addresses this by training models on labeled synthetic data and adapting them to unlabeled real images. While conceptually simple, adaptation is challenging due to the domain gap, i.e., differences in visual appearance and scene structure between synthetic and real data. Prior approaches bridge this gap through pixel-level mixing or feature-level contrastive learning. Yet, these techniques suffer from two major limitations: (1) reliance on high-confidence pseudo-labels restricts learning to a subset of the target domain, and (2) prototype-based contrastive methods initialize class prototypes from source-trained models, yielding biased and unstable anchors during adaptation. To address these issues, we propose a dual-foundation UDA framework that leverages two complementary foundation models. First, we employ the Segment Anything Model (SAM) with superpixel-guided prompting to enable learning from a broader range of target pixels beyond high-confidence predictions. Second, we incorporate DINOv3 to construct stable, domain-invariant class prototypes through its robust representation learning. Our method achieves consistent improvements of +1.3% and +1.4% mIoU over strong UDA baselines on GTA-to-Cityscapes and SYNTHIA-to-Cityscapes, respectively.

</details>


### [22] [SoDa2: Single-Stage Open-Set Domain Adaptation via Decoupled Alignment for Cross-Scene Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03371)
*Yiwen Liu,Minghua Wang,Jing Yao,Xin Zhao,Gemine Vivone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单阶段开放集域适应方法SoDa²，通过解耦对齐策略解决跨场景高光谱图像分类中的域偏移和未知类别识别问题，在三个数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放集跨场景HSI分类方法面临两大挑战：(1) 混合光谱-空间特征的直接对齐导致域偏移问题；(2) 两阶段训练策略带来高计算成本。

Method: 提出SoDa²方法：(1) 贡献感知双模态特征提取，解耦光谱序列信号与空间细节特征；(2) 解耦对齐模块，独立最小化源域和目标域之间的光谱MMD与空间MMD；(3) 单阶段双分支框架，同时学习MMD约束的对齐特征和无约束的固有特征；(4) 高斯混合模型建模两种特征的平方余弦相似度分布，实现无需先验的开放集识别。

Result: 在三组HSI数据集上的大量实验表明，SoDa²在开放集跨场景任务中优于现有最先进方法，具有更高的分类精度和模型迁移性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦光谱-空间特征的对齐以及单阶段训练设计，SoDa²有效解决了域偏移和高计算成本问题，为跨场景HSI开放集分类提供了高效且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-scene hyperspectral image (HSI) classification stands as a fundamental research topic in remote sensing, with extensive applications spanning various fields. Owing to the inclusion of unknown categories in the target domain and the existence of domain shift across different scenes, open-set domain adaptation techniques are commonly employed to address cross-scene HSI classification. However, existing open-set cross-scene HSI classification methods still face two critical challenges: (1) domain shift issues arising from the direct alignment of mixed spectral-spatial features; (2) high computational costs caused by two-stage training strategies. To address these issues, this paper proposes a single-stage open-set domain adaptation method with decoupled alignment (SoDa$^2$) for cross-scene HSI classification. A contribution-aware dual-modality feature extraction is customized to disentangle the characteristics from spectral sequence signals and spatial details, selectively and adaptively enhancing discriminative features. The decoupled alignment module minimizes the Maximum Mean Discrepancy to independently reduce the spectral discrepancy and the spatial discrepancy between the source and target domains, extracting more fine-grained domain-invariant features. A cost-effective single-stage dual-branch framework is designed to learn MMD-constrainted aligned features and constraint-free intrinsic features for adaptive distinction between known and unknown classes. This framework employs a Gaussian Mixture Model to model the squared cosine similarity distribution between the two feature types, enabling open-set recognition without prior knowledge of unknown classes. Extensive experiments on three groups of HSI datasets demonstrate that SoDa$^2$ outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior classification accuracy and model transferability for open-set cross-scene tasks.

</details>


### [23] [Enhancing Self-Supervised Talking Head Forgery Detection via a Training-Free Dual-System Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03390)
*Ke Liu,Jiwei Wei,Shuchang Zhou,Yutong Xiao,Ruikun Chai,Yitong Qin,Yuyang Zhou,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的双系统框架（TFDS），通过借鉴人类认知的双系统理论，利用轻量级阈值路由和细粒度证据引导推理，进一步提升现有基于分数的自监督篡改说话人头像检测器在困难样本上的判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督说话人头像伪造检测器在困难样本上的判别能力有限，表现为不可靠的异常排序，而现有研究主要集中于构建更强的检测器，未能充分挖掘已训练检测器的潜在判别能力。

Method: 提出TFDS框架：将异常评分作为系统1基础，通过轻量级阈值路由将样本分为置信子集和不确定子集；系统2仅对不确定子集进行细粒度证据引导推理，优化原始评分分布中的模糊样本相对排序。

Result: 在多个数据集和扰动设置下取得一致性改进，性能提升主要来源于不确定子集内排序的修正。

Conclusion: 现有自监督说话人头像伪造检测器仍存在未被充分利用的判别线索，通过无需训练的双系统推理可以有效挖掘这些潜在能力。

Abstract: Supervised talking head forgery detection faces severe generalization challenges due to the continuous evolution of generators. By reducing reliance on generator-specific forgery patterns, self-supervised detectors offer stronger cross-generator robustness. However, existing research has mainly focused on building stronger detectors, while the discriminative capacity of trained detectors remains insufficiently exploited. In particular, for score-based self-supervised detectors, the limited discriminative ability on hard cases is often reflected in unreliable anomaly ordering, leaving room for further refinement. Motivated by this observation, we draw inspiration from the dual-system theory of human cognition and propose a Training-Free Dual-System (TFDS) framework to further exploit the latent discriminative capacity of existing score-based self-supervised detectors. TFDS treats anomaly-like scores as the basis of System-1, using lightweight threshold-based routing to partition samples into confident and uncertain subsets. System-2 then revisits only the uncertain subset, performing fine-grained evidence-guided reasoning to refine the relative ordering of ambiguous samples within the original score distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements across datasets and perturbation settings, with the gains arising mainly from corrected ordering within the uncertain subset. These findings show that existing self-supervised talking head forgery detectors still contain underexploited discriminative cues that can be effectively unlocked through training-free dual-system reasoning.

</details>


### [24] [MASRA: MLLM-Assisted Semantic-Relational Consistent Alignment for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03398)
*Ran Ran,Jiwei Wei,Shuchang Zhou,Yitong Qin,Shiyuan He,Zeyu Ma,Yuyang Zhou,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MASRA框架，利用MLLM在训练时生成事件描述和片段字幕两种文本先验，通过事件语义时间对齐（ESTA）和局部关系一致性对齐（LRCA）两种对齐策略，以及解耦对齐交互（DAI）模块，解决视频时间定位中的跨模态语义鸿沟问题，无需在推理时使用MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频时间定位（VTG）面临跨模态语义鸿沟，导致背景特征与查询错误对齐，而直接将查询与时间片段匹配又造成时间语义的判别性和一致性不足。

Method: MASRA框架包含：1) ESTA：将时间上下文与事件语义对齐，增强语义与时间事件的对应关系；2) LRCA：构建文本关系矩阵并与时序特征相似度矩阵对齐，增强时序一致性；3) 语义引导增强与二阶关系注意力两个辅助模块；4) DAI模块：利用上下文感知码本自适应吸收查询无关语义，缓解跨模态鸿沟。

Result: 大量实验表明MASRA优于现有方法，消融研究验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过训练时引入MLLM生成文本先验并进行多粒度语义-时间对齐，能有效提升VTG性能，且不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) faces a cross-modal semantic gap that often leads to background features being incorrectly aligned with the query, while directly matching the query to moments results in insufficient discriminability and consistency of temporal semantics. To address this issue, we propose MLLM-Assisted Semantic-Relational Consistent Alignment (MASRA), a training-time MLLM-based optimization framework for VTG. MASRA leverages an MLLM during training to produce two forms of textual priors, namely event-level descriptions with temporal spans and clip-level captions, and instantiates two MLLM-assisted alignments. Event Semantic Temporal Alignment (ESTA) aligns temporal context with event semantics to explicitly strengthen the correspondence between semantics and temporal events and improve span-level separability. Local Relational Consistency Alignment (LRCA) constructs a textual relation matrix derived from clip-level captions and aligns it with the temporal feature similarity matrix in the model, enhancing temporal consistency while capturing local structural information. MASRA includes two simple supporting modules, semantic-guided enhancement and second-order relational attention, to better utilize the learned semantic context and relational structure. Moreover, we introduce Decoupled Alignment Interaction (DAI) with a context-aware codebook to adaptively absorb query-irrelevant semantics and alleviate the cross-modal gap. The MLLM is only invoked during training and is not used at inference. Extensive experiments show that MASRA outperforms existing methods, and ablation studies validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [25] [GRPO-TTA: Test-Time Visual Tuning for Vision-Language Models via GRPO-Driven Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03403)
*Yujun Li,Hongyuan Zhang,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRPO-TTA，将Group Relative Policy Optimization引入视觉语言模型的测试时适应，通过无监督的组策略优化和定制化奖励函数，在多种分布偏移基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GRPO在视觉语言模型测试时适应中的有效性，解决现有TTA方法在无标签条件下适应不足的问题。

Method: 1) 将类别提示预测重构为组策略优化问题；2) 从CLIP相似度分布中采样top-K类别候选构建输出组，实现无真实标签的概率驱动优化；3) 设计对齐奖励和分散奖励引导视觉编码器调优。

Result: 在多个基准上一致优于现有TTA方法，在自然分布偏移下性能提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: GRPO可有效迁移至TTA场景，基于组策略优化的无监督适应范式在视觉语言模型上具有通用性和优越性。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has recently shown strong performance in post-training large language models and vision-language models. It raises a question of whether the GRPO also significantly promotes the test-time adaptation (TTA) of vision language models. In this paper, we propose Group Relative Policy Optimization for Test-Time Adaptation (GRPO-TTA), which adapts GRPO to the TTA setting by reformulating class-specific prompt prediction as a group-wise policy optimization problem. Specifically, we construct output groups by sampling top-K class candidates from CLIP similarity distributions, enabling probability-driven optimization without access to ground-truth labels. Moreover, we design reward functions tailored to test-time adaptation, including alignment rewards and dispersion rewards, to guide effective visual encoder tuning. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that GRPO-TTA consistently outperforms existing test-time adaptation methods, with notably larger performance gains under natural distribution shifts.

</details>


### [26] [MK-ResRecon: Multi-Kernel Residual Framework for Texture-Aware 3D MRI Refinement from Sparse 2D Slices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03432)
*Prajyot Pyati,Sapna Sachan,Amulya Kumar Mahto,Pranjal Phukan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MK-ResRecon和IdentityRefineNet3D两种模型，从仅12.5%的轴向切片中重建高保真3D MRI体积，实现了快速、无幻觉、可泛化的MRI重建，支持更快的临床成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI采集时间长且患者负担重，长时间扫描易产生运动伪影导致图像质量下降和重复扫描，因此需要从稀疏采样的2D切片中高效重建高质量3D MRI体积。

Method: 提出双模型框架：MK-ResRecon通过多核纹理感知损失预测缺失的中间2D切片；IdentityRefineNet3D将预测切片与原始稀疏切片作为整体3D体积进行精炼，获得平滑的解剖结构。

Result: 在大型T1序列增强后脑部MRI数据集上训练，并在异质性脑部MRI队列上评估，实现了准确、无幻觉、可泛化的3D MRI重建，仅需12.5%的轴向切片。

Conclusion: 该工作为从高度稀疏输入进行3D MRI重建提供了临床验证的框架，为更快、更患者友好的MRI成像开辟了临床可行路径。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) acquisition remains a time-intensive and patient-straining process, as prolonged scan dura- tions increase the likelihood of motion artifacts, which degrade image quality and frequently require repeated scans. To address these chal- lenges, we propose a novel framework with two models MK-ResRecon and IdentityRefineNet3D to reconstruct high-fidelity 3D MRI volumes from sparsely sampled 2D slices-requiring only 12.5% of the axial slices for full resolution 3D reconstruction. MK-ResRecon predicts missing in- termediate 2D slices using a multi-kernel texture-aware loss, preserving fine anatomical details. IdentityRefineNet3D refines the predicted slices and the original sparse slices as a single 3D volume to obtain a smooth anatomical structure. We train the models on a large T1-sequence POST- contrast brain MRI dataset and evaluate on a large heterogeneous brain MRI cohort. The work provides accurate, hallucination-free, generaliz- able and clinically validated framework for 3D MRI reconstruction from highly sparse inputs and enables a clinically viable path towards faster and more patient-friendly MRI imaging.

</details>


### [27] [Learning Discriminative Signed Distance Functions from Multi-scale Level-of-detail Features for 3D Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03437)
*Haibo Xiao,Hanzhe Liang,Jie Zhou,Jinbao Wang,Can Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于表面的3D点云异常检测方法，通过学习多尺度细节层次特征的有符号距离函数来区分正常点和异常点，在Anomaly-ShapeNet和Real3D-AD数据集上分别达到92.1%和85.9%的对象级AUROC，超过当前最优方法2.1%和3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云异常检测面临点云大规模性和稀疏性带来的挑战，现有基于组或点的方法难以学习精确的点级表示。

Method: 提出三种模块：1) 噪声点生成(NPG)模块生成多种类型噪声以暴露异常点；2) 多尺度细节层次特征(MLF)模块捕获从局部细粒度到全局粗粒度的多尺度信息；3) 隐式表面判别(ISD)模块利用多尺度特征学习点云隐式表面表示，训练有符号距离函数区分异常点和正常点。

Result: 在Anomaly-ShapeNet上对象级AUROC达92.1%，在Real3D-AD上达85.9%，分别超过当前最优方法2.1%和3.6%。

Conclusion: 基于表面的有符号距离函数结合多尺度细节层次特征能有效提升3D点云异常检测性能，验证了隐式表面表示在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies from 3D point clouds has received increasing attention in the field of computer vision, with some group-based or point-based methods achieving impressive results in recent years. However, learning accurate point-wise representations for 3D anomaly detection faces great challenges due to the large scale and sparsity of point clouds. In this study, a surface-based method is proposed for 3D anomaly detection, which learns a discriminative signed distance function using multi-scale level-of-detail features. We first present a Noisy Points Generation (NPG) module to generate different types of noise, thereby facilitating the learning of discriminative features by exposing abnormal points. Then, we introduce a Multi-scale Level-of-detail Feature (MLF) module to capture multi-scale information from a point cloud, which provides both fine-grained local and coarse-grained global feature information. Finally, we design an Implicit Surface Discrimination (ISD) module that leverages the extracted multi-scale features to learn an implicit surface representation of point clouds, which effectively trains a signed distance function to distinguish between abnormal and normal points. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves an average object-level AUROC of 92.1\% and 85.9\% on the Anomaly-ShapeNet and Real3D-AD datasets, outperforming the current best approach by 2.1\% and 3.6\%, respectively. Codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DLF-3AD-DA61.

</details>


### [28] [VL-SAM-v3: Memory-Guided Visual Priors for Open-World Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03456)
*Chih-Chung Liu,Zhiwei Lin,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VL-SAM-v3，一个通过检索外部视觉记忆来增强开放世界目标检测的统一框架。该方法为非参数记忆库中检索相关视觉原型，将其转化为稀疏和密集两种互补的视觉先验，并通过记忆引导的提示精炼机制与原始检测提示融合，同时支持开放词汇和开放式推理。在LVIS上的零样本实验表明，该方法显著提升检测性能，尤其在稀有类别上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界目标检测方法主要依赖粗粒度文本语义和参数化知识，对于细粒度外观变化、稀有类别和杂乱场景往往缺乏足够的视觉证据，导致检测性能不足。

Method: 提出VL-SAM-v3框架，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 从非参数记忆库中检索相关视觉原型；(2) 将原型转化为稀疏先验（实例级空间锚定）和密集先验（类别感知局部上下文）；(3) 通过记忆引导的提示精炼机制将两种先验与原始检测提示融合，支持开放词汇和开放端两种推理模式。

Result: 在LVIS数据集上的大量零样本实验表明：(1) VL-SAM-v3在开放词汇和开放端推理下均持续提升检测性能；(2) 在稀有类别上取得显著增益；(3) 在更强的开放词汇检测器SAM3上的实验验证了该检索-精炼机制的通用性。

Conclusion: 检索增强的外部视觉记忆能够有效弥补粗粒度文本语义的不足，为开放世界目标检测提供更丰富的视觉证据；所提出的检索-精炼机制具有良好的通用性，可迁移到不同的检测器架构中。

Abstract: Open-world object detection aims to localize and recognize objects beyond a fixed closed-set label space. It is commonly divided into two categories, i.e., open-vocabulary detection, which assumes a predefined category list at test time, and open-ended detection, which requires generating candidate categories during the inference. Existing methods rely primarily on coarse textual semantics and parametric knowledge, which often provide insufficient visual evidence for fine-grained appearance variation, rare categories, and cluttered scenes. In this paper, we propose VL-SAM-v3, a unified framework that augments open-world detection with retrieval-grounded external visual memory. Specifically, once candidate categories are available, VL-SAM-v3 retrieves relevant visual prototypes from a non-parametric memory bank and transforms them into two complementary visual priors, i.e., sparse priors for instance-level spatial anchoring and dense priors for class-aware local context. These priors are integrated with the original detection prompts via Memory-Guided Prompt Refinement, enabling a shared retrieval-and-refinement mechanism that supports open-vocabulary and open-ended inference.Extensive zero-shot experiments on LVIS show that VL-SAM-v3 consistently improves detection performance under both open-vocabulary and open-ended inference, with particularly strong gains on rare categories.Moreover, experiments with a stronger open-vocabulary detector (i.e., SAM3) validate the generality of the proposed retrieval-and-refinement mechanism.

</details>


### [29] [First Shape, Then Meaning: Efficient Geometry and Semantics Learning for Indoor Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03463)
*Remi Chierchia,Léo Lebrat,David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Olivier Salvado,Clinton Fookes,Rodrigo Santa Cruz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FSTM提出了一种统一的神经表面重建方法，通过几何预热（仅使用RGB和几何线索）与后续语义场估计的两阶段流程，在无需复杂多SDF设计的情况下，实现了比标准联合优化和现有多SDF方法更快、更鲁棒的几何与语义重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经表面重建方法虽然能整合语义标签，但通常采用多SDF学习范式，存在训练速度慢、可扩展性有限的问题。作者旨在探索是否可以通过简化的单阶段流程（而非复杂多SDF设计）同时实现高质量的几何与语义重建。

Method: FSTM采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段（几何预热）仅使用RGB输入和几何线索（如深度、法线）优化场景几何，不使用任何语义监督；第二阶段再进行语义场估计。该方法不使用专门化模块或复杂的多SDF设计，而是采用精简的公式化表达。

Result: 在Replica数据集上训练速度提升2.3倍；在ScanNet++真实数据集上对真实世界不完美性具有更强鲁棒性；通过恢复场景中更多物体的表面，实现了更高的召回率；在合成和真实室内数据集上均优于多SDF方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在神经表面重建中，先优化几何再学习语义的两阶段策略比标准的联合优化更有效，且精简的单一模型设计即可达到甚至超越复杂多SDF方法的效果，为室内3D重建提供了更高效、可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Neural Surface Reconstruction has become a standard methodology for indoor 3D reconstruction, with Signed Distance Functions (SDFs) proving particularly effective for representing scene geometry. A variety of applications require a detailed understanding of the scene context, driving the need for object-level semantic signals. While recent methods successfully integrate semantic labels, they often inherit the slow training time and limited scalability of multi-SDF learning. In this paper, we introduce FSTM, a unified approach for learning geometry and semantics through a two-step process: a geometry warm-up using RGB inputs and geometric cues, followed by semantic field estimation. By first optimising geometry without semantic supervision, we observe substantial improvements compared to the standard joint optimisation. Rather than relying on specialised modules or complex multi-SDF designs, FSTM shows that a streamlined formulation is sufficient to achieve strong geometric and semantic reconstructions. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world indoor datasets show that our method outperforms multi-SDF approaches. It trains 2.3x faster on Replica, improves robustness to real-world imperfections on ScanNet++, and achieves higher recall by recovering the surfaces of more objects in the scene. The code will be made available at https://remichierchia.github.io/FSTM.

</details>


### [30] [MHPR: Multidimensional Human Perception and Reasoning Benchmark for Large Vision-Languate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03485)
*Kangkang Wang,Qinting Jiang,Wanping Zhang,Bowen Ren,Shengzhao Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MHPR是一个面向人类中心场景的联合感知-推理基准，包含多级数据设计和自动化标注流水线，通过SFT和RL训练显著提升Qwen2.5-VL-7B在细粒度属性与高层语义上的表现，达到与大型模型相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LVLM基准多聚焦于单任务设置，缺乏细粒度的、以人为中心的评估，无法满足电影分析、虚拟数字人等实际应用中对多维人类理解的需求。

Method: 提出MHPR基准，包含四级数据设计（C-RD、SFT-D、RL-D、T-D）和自动化标注流水线ACVG（类别属性分解、属性特定重写、多模型投票）。在细粒度属性（外观、服饰、姿态、部位）和高层语义（社会关系、动作语义、空间关系、意图与功能）上评估SOTA模型。

Result: 1）格式对齐的SFT数据显著提升指令遵循与稳定性；2）基于坏例分析的挑战聚焦RL数据增强困难样本的感知与推理；3）Qwen2.5-VL-7B在MHPR上训练后性能大幅提升，与更大模型性能接近。

Conclusion: MHPR和ACVG为可复现、可扩展的人类中心感知与推理研究提供了高质量基准和自动化工具。

Abstract: Multidimensional human understanding is essential for real-world applications such as film analysis and virtual digital humans, yet current LVLM benchmarks largely focus on single-task settings and lack fine-grained, human-centric evaluation. In this work, we introduce MHPR, a comprehensive benchmark for joint perception-reasoning over human-centric scenes spanning individual, multi-person, and human-object interaction dimensions. MHPR comprises a multi-level data design-Captioned Raw Data (C-RD), Supervised Fine-Tuning Data (SFT-D), Reinforcement Learning Data (RL-D), and Test Data (T-D)-together with an automated caption/VQA generation pipeline (ACVG) that performs category-wise attribute decomposition, attribute-specific rewriting, and multi-model voting to ensure high-quality, scalable annotations. We evaluate state-of-the-art vision-language models on fine-grained attributes (appearance, clothing, pose, parts) and high-level semantics (social relations, action semantics, spatial relations, intent and functionality). Our findings show that: 1) format-aligned SFT data substantially improves instruction following and stability; 2) challenge-focused RL data derived from bad-case analysis further enhances perception and reasoning on difficult instances; and 3) training Qwen2.5-VL-7B with MHPR yields significant gains, achieving near-parity with considerably larger models. We release ACVG and MHPR to facilitate reproducible, extensible research on human-centric perception and reasoning.

</details>


### [31] [BFORE: Butterfly-Firefly Optimized Retinex Enhancement for Low-Light Image Quality Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03509)
*Ahmed Cherif*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BFORE框架，融合蝴蝶优化算法与萤火虫算法自动优化Retinex增强管道参数，在无训练数据情况下PSNR达17.22dB，SSIM达0.5417，超越RetinexNet等深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Retinex方法依赖人工调参，无法泛化至多样光照条件；深度学习虽有效但需要大量训练数据且泛化受限。

Method: 将图像转换至HSV空间，对亮度通道进行AGCWD自适应伽马校正与自适应去噪；使用蝴蝶优化算法优化MSRCR参数，萤火虫算法优化AGCWD与去噪参数，并通过混合BOA-FA切换策略平衡全局探索与局部开发。

Result: 在LOL数据集上PSNR达17.22dB，较直方图均衡化提升20.3%，较MSRCR提升17.5%；平均亮度129.97最接近理想中间值；比RetinexNet的PSNR(16.77dB)和SSIM(0.5417 vs 0.4252)更优，且无需训练数据；混合优化带来12.3% PSNR和14.8% SSIM提升。

Conclusion: BFORE通过元启发式优化有效提升了Retinex管道的自适应性与增强质量，在无需训练数据的情况下达到甚至超越深度学习方法，为低光照增强提供了轻量级且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement is a fundamental challenge in computer vision and multimedia applications, as images captured under insufficient illumination suffer from poor visibility, low contrast, and color distortion. Existing Retinex-based methods rely on manually tuned parameters that fail to generalize across diverse lighting conditions. This paper proposes BFORE (Butterfly-Firefly Optimized Retinex Enhancement), a novel hybrid metaheuristic-optimized framework that automatically tunes the parameters of a multi-stage Retinex-based pipeline. The proposed method converts the input image to HSV color space and applies Adaptive Gamma Correction with Weighted Distribution (AGCWD) to the luminance channel, followed by adaptive denoising. A Butterfly Optimization Algorithm (BOA) optimizes the Multi-Scale Retinex with Color Restoration (MSRCR) parameters, while a Firefly Algorithm (FA) optimizes the AGCWD and denoising parameters. A hybrid BOA-FA switching strategy dynamically balances global exploration and local exploitation. Experimental evaluation on the LOL benchmark dataset (15 paired test images) demonstrates that BFORE achieves the highest PSNR (17.22 dB) among all traditional enhancement methods, with 20.3% improvement over Histogram Equalization and 17.5% over MSRCR. BFORE produces the most naturally balanced mean brightness (129.97), closest to the ideal mid-tone value. Notably, BFORE outperforms RetinexNet -- a deep learning baseline -- in both PSNR (17.22 vs. 16.77 dB) and SSIM (0.5417 vs. 0.4252) without requiring any training data. The hybrid BOA-FA optimization contributes a 12.3% PSNR improvement and 14.8% SSIM improvement over the unoptimized pipeline.

</details>


### [32] [DALPHIN: Benchmarking Digital Pathology AI Copilots Against Pathologists on an Open Multicentric Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03544)
*Carlijn Lems,Sander Moonemans,Natálie Klubíčková,Biagio Brattoli,Taebum Lee,Seokhwi Kim,Veronica Vilaplana,Laura Pons,Sapir Hochman,Mauricio Eduardo Suárez-Franck,Pedro Luis Fernandez,Julius Drachneris,Donatas Petroska,Renaldas Augulis,Arvydas Laurinavicius,Domingos Oliveira,Diana Montezuma,Anouk B. Bouwmeester,Dominique van Midden,Anne-Marie Vos,Shoko Vos,Jolique van Ipenburg,Maschenka Balkenhol,Koen Winkler,Iris Nagtegaal,Konnie Hebeda,Uta Flucke,Katrien Grünberg,Josef Skopal,Brinder S. Chohan,Jordi Temprana-Salvador,Enrico Munari,Luca Cima,Giulia Querzoli,Yosamin Gonzalez Belisario,Jaeike W. Faber,Geert J. L. H. van Leenders,Jan H. von der Thüsen,Lodewijk A. A. Brosens,Ronald R. de Krijger,Pieter Wesseling,Sandrine Florquin,Mateusz Maniewski,Adam Kowalewski,Robert Barna,Dina Tiniakos,Joan Lop Gros,Rogier Donders,Jake S. F. Maurits,Ming Yang Lu,Chengkuan Chen,Faisal Mahmood,Jeroen van der Laak,Nadieh Khalili,Frédérique Meeuwsen,Francesco Ciompi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DALPHIN是首个多中心开放病理AI辅助诊断基准，包含来自300个病例的1236张图像，覆盖130种诊断、6个国家和14个亚专科。基于31名病理学家的人类表现基准，评估了GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro和PathChat+三种AI模型。PathChat在4/6任务中达到专家水平，Gemini为2/6，GPT为1/6。数据集和评估平台已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具备视觉问答能力的病理基础模型涌现，需要独立、公正的基准测试来评估这些AI辅助工具在常规病理诊断中的实际潜力，以确保其安全性和有效性。

Method: 创建DALPHIN多中心开放基准，包含1236张图像（来自300个病例，涵盖130种诊断、6个国家、14个亚专科）；招募31名病理学家（来自10个国家、不同专业水平）建立人类表现基准；评估GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro和PathChat+三种模型在顺序和独立两种答案生成模式下的表现。

Result: PathChat在6项任务中有4项达到专家水平（无统计学显著差异），Gemini在2/6任务中达到，GPT在1/6任务中达到。未见任何模型在所有任务中达到专家水平。

Conclusion: DALPHIN基准表明病理专用AI辅助模型（PathChat+）在多项任务中接近专家水平，但通用模型表现较弱。公开数据集和评估平台有助于推动该领域的稳健、持久评估。

Abstract: Foundation models with visual question answering capabilities for digital pathology are emerging. Such unprecedented technology requires independent benchmarking to assess its potential in assisting pathologists in routine diagnostics. We created DALPHIN, the first multicentric open benchmark for pathology AI copilots, comprising 1236 images from 300 cases, spanning 130 rare to common diagnoses, 6 countries, and 14 subspecialties. The DALPHIN design and dataset are introduced alongside a human performance benchmark of 31 pathologists from 10 countries with varying expertise. We report results for two general-purpose (GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro) and one pathology-specific copilot (PathChat+) for sequential and independent answer generation. We observed no statistically significant difference from expert-level performance in four of six tasks for PathChat, 2/6 tasks for Gemini, and 1/6 tasks for GPT. DALPHIN is publicly released with sequestered, indirectly accessible ground truth to foster robust and enduring benchmarking. Data, methods, and the evaluation platform are accessible through dalphin.grand-challenge.org.

</details>


### [33] [Erase Persona, Forget Lore: Benchmarking Multimodal Copyright Unlearning in Large Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03547)
*JuneHyoung Kwon,JungMin Yun,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CoVUBench基准，首个专门用于评估大型视觉-语言模型中版权内容遗忘效果的框架，使用合成数据和系统化视觉变体来评估遗忘泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型可能记忆并再生受版权保护的视觉内容（如角色、标识），现有评估方法缺乏鲁棒性，无法捕捉跨模态概念擦除的细微差异，亟需标准化的评估工具。

Method: 利用程序生成的合法安全合成数据，结合系统化的视觉变体（包括合成变化和多领域展现），设计全面的多模态评估协议，从版权持有者角度衡量遗忘效果，从部署者角度衡量模型通用能力保持。

Result: CoVUBench能够严格衡量遗忘效果与模型通用能力之间的权衡关系，为LVLM负责任的遗忘方法开发提供标准化评估工具。

Conclusion: CoVUBench是首个专门评估LVLM中版权内容遗忘的基准，填补了该领域评估方法的空白，有望推动负责任且有效的遗忘方法发展。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), trained on web-scale data, risk memorizing and regenerating copyrighted visual content such as characters and logos, creating significant challenges. Machine unlearning offers a path to mitigate these risks by removing specific content post-training, but evaluating its effectiveness, especially in the complex multimodal setting of LVLMs, remains an open problem. Current evaluation methods often lack robustness or fail to capture the nuances of cross-modal concept erasure. To address this critical gap, we introduce the CoVUBench benchmark, the first framework specifically designed for evaluating copyright content unlearning in LVLMs. CoVUBench utilizes procedurally generated, legally safe synthetic data coupled with systematic visual variations spanning compositional changes and diverse domain manifestations to ensure realistic and robust evaluation of unlearning generalization. Our comprehensive multimodal evaluation protocol assesses both forgetting efficacy from the copyright holder perspective and the preservation of general model utility from the deployer viewpoint. By rigorously measuring this crucial trade-off, CoVUBench provides a standardized tool to advance the development of responsible and effective unlearning methods for LVLMs.

</details>

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Grounded Correspondence框架，利用自监督视觉backbone的实例判别特征，通过确定性二分图匹配替代传统学习型动力学模块来维持视频目标中心学习中的时间一致性，无需任何可学习参数进行时序建模，在MOVi-D、MOVi-E和YouTube-VIS上取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频目标中心学习方法依赖学习型动力学模块来预测未来目标表示（slots），但这些方法本质上是对离散对应问题的昂贵近似，计算效率低且复杂度高。

Method: 利用现代自监督视觉backbone中编码的实例判别特征，从冻结backbone特征中的显著区域初始化slots；通过匈牙利匹配算法在slot表示上维持帧间身份对应，完全替代学习型的时序过渡函数。

Result: 在MOVi-D、MOVi-E和YouTube-VIS数据集上取得了与现有方法竞争的优越性能，且时序建模部分零可学习参数。

Conclusion: 自监督视觉backbone已具备足够的实例判别能力，通过确定性的离散匹配（匈牙利算法）即可高效解决视频目标的时间对应问题，无需复杂的学习型时序预测模块。

Abstract: The de facto approach in video object-centric learning maintains temporal consistency through learned dynamics modules that predict future object representations, called slots. We demonstrate that these predictors function as expensive approximations of discrete correspondence problems. Modern self-supervised vision backbones already encode instance-discriminative features that distinguish objects reliably. Exploiting these features eliminates the need for learned temporal prediction. We introduce Grounded Correspondence, a framework that replaces learned transition functions with deterministic bipartite matching. Slots initialize from salient regions in frozen backbone features. Frame-to-frame identity is maintained through Hungarian matching on slot representations. The approach requires zero learnable parameters for temporal modeling yet achieves competitive performance on MOVi-D, MOVi-E, and YouTube-VIS. Project page: https://magenta-sherbet-85b101.netlify.app/

</details>


### [35] [AniMatrix: An Anime Video Generation Model that Thinks in Art, Not Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03652)
*Tencent HY Team*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AniMatrix是一个面向动漫艺术而非物理真实性的视频生成模型，通过双通道条件机制和三阶段训练（重新定义正确性、覆盖物理先验、区分艺术与失败），在五项专业评估指标中四项排名第一，最大提升为提示理解（+22.4%）和艺术运动（+16.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型内化物理真实感为先验，但动漫故意违背物理规律（如拖尾、冲击帧、Q版变形），其数千种共存的艺术惯例导致没有单一的"动漫物理"可供模型学习。物理偏置的模型要么扁平化定义动漫的艺术性，要么在其风格变异性下崩溃。

Method: 1) 双通道条件机制：Production Knowledge System将动漫编码为结构化分类学（风格、运动、相机、VFX），AniCaption从像素推断这些变量；可训练的标签编码器保留场值结构，冻结的T5编码器处理自由形式文本，通过交叉注意力（细粒度控制）和AdaLN调制（全局执行）双路径注入。2) 三阶段训练课程：风格-运动-变形课程，从近物理运动过渡到完全动漫表现力。3) 变形感知偏好优化：使用领域特定奖励模型区分有意艺术性与病态崩溃。

Result: 在由专业动画师评分的五维度动漫特定人工评估中，AniMatrix在五项中的四项排名第一，相对于Seedance-Pro 1.0的最大提升在提示理解（+0.70，+22.4%）和艺术运动（+0.55，+16.9%）上。

Conclusion: AniMatrix通过结构化知识编码、渐进式课程学习和偏好优化，成功实现了以艺术正确性而非物理正确性为目标的动漫视频生成，显著超越了现有模型。将公开发布模型权重和推理代码。

Abstract: Video generation models internalize physical realism as their prior. Anime deliberately violates physics: smears, impact frames, chibi shifts; and its thousands of coexisting artistic conventions yield no single "physics of anime" a model can absorb. Physics-biased models therefore flatten the artistry that defines the medium or collapse under its stylistic variance. We present AniMatrix, a video generation model that targets artistic rather than physical correctness through a dual-channel conditioning mechanism and a three-step transition: redefine correctness, override the physics prior, and distinguish art from failure. First, a Production Knowledge System encodes anime as a structured taxonomy of controllable production variables (Style, Motion, Camera, VFX), and AniCaption infers these variables from pixels as directorial directives. A trainable tag encoder preserves the field-value structure of this taxonomy while a frozen T5 encoder handles free-form narrative; dual-path injection (cross-attention for fine-grained control, AdaLN modulation for global enforcement) ensures categorical directives are never diluted by open-ended text. Second, a style-motion-deformation curriculum transitions the model from near-physical motion to full anime expressiveness. Third, deformation-aware preference optimization with a domain-specific reward model separates intentional artistry from pathological collapse. On an anime-specific human evaluation with five production dimensions scored by professional animators, AniMatrix ranks first on four of five, with the largest gains over Seedance-Pro 1.0 on Prompt Understanding (+0.70, +22.4 percent) and Artistic Motion (+0.55, +16.9 percent). We will publicly release the AniMatrix model weights and inference code.

</details>

*Lingyi Hong,Jinglun Li,Xinyu Zhou,Kaixun Jiang,Pinxue Guo,Zhaoyu Chen,Runze Li,Xingdong Sheng,Wenqiang Zhang*
